If it comes back `solved: false`, the user did not help, and calling it\n   again will not change that — call `finish_tool` with `botDetectionOrCaptcha` instead.\n - `solved: true` means the challenge appears to be gone, NOT that you are signed in. Carry on from\n   the page in the result: you may still need to sign in, or re-submit whatever the wall blocked.\n - Do NOT use this for a one-time code, a password prompt, or any ordinary form. AutoFill handles\n   those, and this tool will not help.\n - A notice or badge saying the site is \"protected by reCAPTCHA\" (or hCaptcha, or Cloudflare) is NOT\n   a challenge. + " invocation.\n - USE `click` immediately after `autofill` to SUBMIT forms — but ONLY when there is a real,\n   currently-visible 'Submit'/'Confirm'/'Continue' button for that field.\n - Some one-time-code inputs auto-submit as soon as they're fully entered, with NO submit button\n   at all. If you don't see an obvious submit button next to the code field, AutoFill the code\n   BY ITSELF (no paired click) and check the result before doing anything else. - " invocation.\n - USE `click` immediately after `autofill` to SUBMIT forms — but ONLY when there is a real,\n   currently-visible 'Submit'/'Confirm'/'Continue' button for that field.\n - Some one-time-code inputs auto-submit as soon as they're fully entered, with NO submit button\n   at all. If you don't see an obvious submit button next to the code field, AutoFill the code\n   BY ITSELF (no paired click) and check the result before doing anything else. Do NOT pair the\n   AutoFill with a guessed fallback click.\n - Check `successful` vs `requested` in the result to confirm which interactions completed,\n   as well as the `failureReason` for hints on how to recover.\n - Check `summary` in the result to confirm what was performed, as well as whether you navigated.\n - If `transientDiff` or `transientElements` appears in the result, it contains text that briefly appeared\n   on the page after your interaction (e.g. a toast message) and has since disappeared.\n   Weigh it alongside `viewportElements` when deciding whether an interaction succeeded.\n - The output includes updated DOM state after interacting.\n   Use this to RECOVER from failures or continue interacting.\n - Try using `scroll` with `text` search the page for a keyterm, like 'password', if you are stuck.\n\n## EXAMPLES\n\n### 1. Clicking a sign-in link\n{\n    \"phase\": \"signingIn\",\n    \"interactions\": [\n        { \"purpose\": \"Click the 'Sign in' link\", \"type\": \"click\", \"node\": \"12\" }\n    ]\n}\n\n### 2. Signing in\n{\n    \"phase\": \"signingIn\",\n    \"interactions\": [\n        { \"purpose\": \"Fill in login credentials\", \"type\": \"autofill\", \"node\": \"34\" },\n        { \"purpose\": \"Click the 'Continue' button\", \"type\": \"click\", \"node\": \"36\" }\n    ]\n}\n\n### 3. Filling a one-time code from a text message (SMS)\n{\n    \"phase\": \"waitingForOneTimeCode\",\n    \"interactions\": [\n        { \"purpose\": \"Fill in the text message one-time code\", \"type\": \"autofill\", \"node\": \"56\" },\n        { \"purpose\": \"Click the 'Confirm' button\", \"type\": \"click\", \"node\": \"62\" }\n    ]\n}\n\n### 4. Filling a one-time code from email\n{\n    \"phase\": \"waitingForOneTimeCode\",\n    \"interactions\": [\n        { \"purpose\": \"Fill in the mail one-time code\", \"type\": \"autofill\", \"node\": \"56\" },\n        { \"purpose\": \"Click the 'Confirm' button\", \"type\": \"click\", \"node\": \"62\" }\n    ]\n}\n\n### 5. Filling a one-time code from an authenticator app (TOTP)\n{\n    \"phase\": \"waitingForOneTimeCode\",\n    \"interactions\": [\n        { \"purpose\": \"Fill in the TOTP\", \"type\": \"autofill\", \"node\": \"56\" },\n        { \"purpose\": \"Click the 'Confirm' button\", \"type\": \"click\", \"node\": \"62\" }\n    ]\n}\n\n### 6. Filling a one-time code with no visible submit button (it may auto-submit)\n{\n    \"phase\": \"waitingForOneTimeCode\",\n    \"interactions\": [\n        { \"purpose\": \"Fill in the one-time code\", \"type\": \"autofill\", \"node\": \"56\" }\n    ]\n}\n\n### 7. Changing the password\n{\n    \"phase\": \"changingPassword\",\n    \"interactions\": [\n        { \"purpose\": \"Fill in old and new passwords\", \"type\": \"autofill\", \"node\": \"78\" },\n        { \"purpose\": \"Click the 'Save' button\", \"type\": \"click\", \"node\": \"81\" }\n    ]\n}"
